Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a formal_languages algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 661. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 239/3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the formal_languages decomposition. By minimality, the element a^661 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 239-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the formal_languages object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 797. The moduli space has dimension at most 838 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to formal_languages theory, we decompose the problem into 797 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 838. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
